## Native accessors, dump completion and transaction callers checkpoint, 2026-10-09

- Nine assigned current identities/complete deltas revalidated; HEAD unchanged. Thirteen actual current physical path bindings follow, including whole539line transaction and whole earlier graphics-lock plan; remaining paths explicitly partial. Native window files end230; only actual214-230 saved, not printed out-of-range blank lines
- Paired native menu snapshots copy borrowed title/subtitle and scroll/wrapping counts; listbox title borrowed, paired window snapshot borroweddata/callback. INTROSPECT_ON gates match shared adapters; no owning lifetime/synchronization implied by snapshot naming. Preserve narrow paired accessors/desktop wrapping defaults
- Actual sampling captures atomic request generation before allocating/reading/scanning framebuffer, publishes sampled generation last. Actual dump chooses sampled generation when visible, request generation when hidden; it marks dumpgeneration consumed before get_state/allocation/write/flush/close/rename succeed. Keep improvement preserving newer requests, reconcile terminal failure/retry/publication with existing GQR0173/BR0233 owners before any new root. Current temp rename checks flush/close but fputs return is not directly checked
- Actual main Activity JNI calls full get_state directly; request/path JNI also supports level preview. Engine event loops call dump during hidden sleep or after flip; direct JNI state construction remains separate ownership/containment path. No race/runtime proof or generalized accessor lifetime finding from getter alone
- Whole current graphics transaction validates target count/update count<=64/unique valid keys, reads/stages all files before publishing, chains up to64 bounded file edits, keeps original bytes for rollback, creates O_EXCL private names and syncs files/parents. Retained-backup and independent close-after-fsync repairs remain present; preserve completed GQR0128/0129 acceptance rather than reopen from old descriptions
- Actual safety snapshot patch holds checked store guard, normalizes snapshot, builds config path list and five-key updates before batch. Journal write uses atomic replace; first-run marker replaces offered bytes before preview acceptance. Native option mirror holds process mutex but invokes transaction even when safety lock acquisition returnsnull, exactly documented in earlier0104 lock-admission plan. Reuse existing BR0029 serialized publication/affinity acceptance; no duplicate owner or observed corruption claim
- Additional transaction ownership edge for later contract/canonical reconciliation: write_private_file leaves candidate private_path populated before O_EXCL acquisition succeeds; cleanup removes nonempty temporary/backup paths. Inspect ordinary name/collision/error contracts and maintained fixture coverage before inferring acquired ownership from name uniqueness. No filesystem/security/fault probe executed or new finding admitted
- Gate1 complete; next current GLES/projection callers and maintained fixtures/registration, fresh completed/open canonical owners and full context-diff reconciliation, then consolidation/report/import/independent audit. No product/test/script edits/new status/rating/runtime claim;0116 audit debt remains open, overall goal active

  {

    "read_lines": [
      {
        "line": 2261,
        "text": "### BR-0200: P2 - Publish complete mirrored graphics config updates",
        "line_lf_sha256": "3ca619e17fd38a9423916ccd78d510be43bf07bffe9aa61c84caf9011ae157d5"
      },
      {
        "line": 2262,
        "text": "",
        "line_lf_sha256": "01ba4719c80b6fe911b091a7c05124b64eeece964e09c058ef8f9805daca546b"
      },
      {
        "line": 2263,
        "text": "- [x] FIXED",
        "line_lf_sha256": "7face513eb717307cfe301caa55c9b6fb7a59f43f40d11e6253e8979632146f8"
      },
      {
        "line": 2264,
        "text": "- Type: defect",
        "line_lf_sha256": "c35c251f5479984ecc3bbf292b2b96bf30c6e0e55e1ed672b025c4eb0125e918"
      },
      {
        "line": 2265,
        "text": "- Confidence: high",
        "line_lf_sha256": "666adfafe63b7fd4e579910b19eecf81f6e25bc211a7165f4bffdf849ca7e334"
      },
      {
        "line": 2266,
        "text": "- Category: correctness/data-loss",
        "line_lf_sha256": "1794d615bc8b1783b33eabc6b80f883e060c4280c21c83a18a08faf8099af6d8"
      },
      {
        "line": 2267,
        "text": "- Found by: R1-CHUNK-0103",
        "line_lf_sha256": "d31c91795016f9a9389d2a1e7f0dd343cff180a9acc7cabada4be208b6b41f05"
      },
      {
        "line": 2268,
        "text": "- Location: `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/app/src/main/cpp/shared/android_graphics_options.c:L72-L178` in config patching and mirroring",
        "line_lf_sha256": "4f7f9b2dfaecbde6d136ee3bdb2411ce00ac87d329ce2687c68a12823822282e"
      },
      {
        "line": 2269,
        "text": "- Related: `android/app/src/main/cpp/shared/android_graphics_options.c:L214-L220,L230-L353` and `android/app/src/main/java/com/dxxredux/app/SetupConfigFiles.kt:L63-L79`",
        "line_lf_sha256": "7f2c8998c1d0e8c2c7c32670557328aa452f0ef8e8ff09998ba0cf37e91ad892"
      },
      {
        "line": 2270,
        "text": "- Evidence: `patch_config_file` reconstructs each existing file in a fixed 32,768-byte stack buffer. `append_buf` silently drops any complete line or replacement that would make the retained length reach 32,768 bytes, but the helper then opens the public path with `\"w\"` and replaces the original with that incomplete buffer. It also treats a read failure as an empty file, publishes directly rather than through a sibling temporary file, ignores `fwrite`, `fflush`, `fsync`, and `fclose` results, and returns no status. `mirror_config_key` repeats this independently for root and each existing D1/D2 directory, so interruption or one target failure can leave three advertised app-wide defaults at different generations while the setter still reports success.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "6ac29a79035ba0817ad9692e33675593cd754c7dcaebe8e17c2279e75ac60acf"
      },
      {
        "line": 2271,
        "text": "- Trigger: Persist a graphics option when any mirrored `descent.cfg` contains more than 32,767 retained bytes, when its read fails transiently but writing succeeds, when storage fills during replacement, or when the process is interrupted after truncation or between mirror targets",
        "line_lf_sha256": "2c8913041e27ce978af4574fd0506e69e123451a177834b5ebe0a4649dfca24a"
      },
      {
        "line": 2272,
        "text": "- Impact: Unrelated configuration lines can be silently deleted, an existing config can become empty or partial, and root, D1, and D2 can disagree even though the in-game or overlay edit appears successful. Later launches can lose music, display, player, or other settings and select different graphics defaults by game.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "4a06a8b8d6c67c6455f38c77cecb3f9ba048b1ee21ede482e7098cf930ea4f84"
      },
      {
        "line": 2273,
        "text": "- Expected: Patching preserves the complete prior file except for the named key, validates every I/O step, publishes only a complete durable replacement, and exposes any failed or partial multi-file mirror to the caller without destroying the last valid files.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "ae77d9a9c02ec8740217c9e6a778816627d0558f4357d1f5e9ef277446edf768"
      },
      {
        "line": 2274,
        "text": "- Suggested fix: Replace the fixed buffer with a checked dynamic or streaming parser that has an explicit supported-size policy. Write each result to a uniquely claimed sibling temporary file, flush, sync, close, and atomically rename only after success; stage all intended targets before publication and retain or restore prior files if the mirror transaction cannot complete. Return a typed result through the setter and JNI or menu caller.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "09fa917f3faf5131472ef2849e2b08644ba0ac70d57326ef4b98f8cd452fefec"
      },
      {
        "line": 2275,
        "text": "- Validation: Patch files at 32,766, 32,767, 32,768, and larger lengths, with long and unterminated lines, duplicate and absent keys, and unrelated unknown keys; inject read, allocation, short-write, flush, sync, close, rename, interruption, and second- or third-target failures. Assert exact preservation or an explicit failure with every prior file intact, then verify successful root/D1/D2 mirrors survive restart.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "e122617d009d92c3900a7d30450824769ff8ef94ad50833132e78701b7844089"
      },
      {
        "line": 2276,
        "text": "- Resolution: Fixed on 2026-07-31. Native graphics persistence now uses the standalone `graphics_config_transaction` component instead of a fixed buffer or `WriteConfigFile`. It reads every intended root, D1, and D2 target completely under an explicit 16 MiB per-file limit; constructs complete replacements without a line-length limit; stages uniquely claimed sibling files and prior-generation backups; checks write, flush, file sync, close, replacement, and parent-directory sync; and restores every already-published target when a later step fails. Graphics setters return `OK`, `UNKNOWN`, or `PERSIST_FAILED`, JNI returns that typed result, and failures are routed to the graphics debug log. Launcher and combined-import updates now use `AtomicFilePublication.writeUtf8Batch`, which stages every complete UTF-8 config and prior generation before publishing and restores or removes earlier targets after a later failure. Regressions cover 32,766, 32,767, 32,768, 65,537, and over-limit files; long and unterminated lines; duplicate, absent, and unknown keys; read, allocation, write, flush, sync, close, and every replace position; root/D1/D2 success; large launcher content; existing-target rollback; and newly created target removal. The focused native and 11-test graphics suites, all 21 native CTests, all 573 JVM tests with one skip, scoped code quality, Android debug assembly for arm64-v8a, armeabi-v7a, and x86_64, and `git diff --check` passed.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "f02eb8df03b57b061351cb028fa665e4f0416e1d686715363145b3c9ada5c092"
      },
      {
        "line": 2277,
        "text": "",
        "line_lf_sha256": "01ba4719c80b6fe911b091a7c05124b64eeece964e09c058ef8f9805daca546b"
      }
    ]

      },
      {
        "line": 18717,
        "text": "- Found by: R1-CHUNK-0492",
        "line_lf_sha256": "8ef350a5ddcf7ff5c992b3a9f9003111db556d9a38187300eb2142cae1ae70e7"
      },
      {
        "line": 18718,
        "text": "- Location: `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/helpers/introspect.sh:L36-L52,L85-L101` in game and setup snapshot acquisition",
        "line_lf_sha256": "3db39d3bccb69204ac24731cb1effdd75faf5347a02db78b2afb55762f75af9f"
      },
      {
        "line": 18719,
        "text": "- Related: `android/app/src/main/java/com/dxxredux/app/MainActivity.kt:L2301-L2310`; `android/app/src/main/cpp/shared/game_introspect.cpp:L2106-L2149`; `d1/arch/sdl/event.c:L258`; `d2/arch/sdl/event.c:L258`; BR-0190",
        "line_lf_sha256": "56dddc389762e3ce7ee52a63a9421d0c51defdae4dd4866a8ac89f5531c8c9c7"
      },
      {
        "line": 18720,
        "text": "- Evidence: Both acquisition paths send a broadcast, sleep exactly one second, and accept whichever final-path JSON already exists. They neither remove or timestamp the prior file nor include a request identifier in the broadcast and result. MainActivity ignores the game request while `gameStarted` is false and otherwise only sets a volatile flag for `game_introspect_check_and_dump` on a later engine event; broadcast success therefore proves neither receiver admission nor publication. A paused, blocked, slow, stopped, or just-starting game can leave the previous file untouched past the fixed sleep, and the helper then presents that stale generation as current. Setup uses the same uncorrelated fixed-delay contract.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "5e74cc53882132b674d42d7bcd0cdac5271a3972e0d0248ab8946a22703494a9"
      },
      {
        "line": 18721,
        "text": "- Trigger: Leave a prior valid introspection file, then request another snapshot while the game is before `gameStarted`, paused, blocked for more than one second, stopped before its next event tick, or while SetupActivity does not service the matching request",
        "line_lf_sha256": "7f1b6c3a39154446679f64c999275ab7cfc3cf8522a4cd3c3b2b0757b7046d19"
      },
      {
        "line": 18722,
        "text": "- Impact: Developers and automated agents can diagnose menus, player state, position, setup readiness, or console output from an earlier frame or session while believing it reflects the just-issued request, leading to false test conclusions and incorrect follow-up actions",
        "line_lf_sha256": "d0f457ee6e315ee07760a25c0bd58d88c05d674d925155d1f698604ded9f8e18"
      },
      {
        "line": 18723,
        "text": "- Expected: A successful helper response proves that the requested component admitted this invocation and atomically published a snapshot generated after it, or fails with a bounded precise timeout",
        "line_lf_sha256": "8f9ab2d8a36bc5956ded8197d5632e1c2dd81ff36f8c436ad0bdf11fe7e28f23"
      },
      {
        "line": 18724,
        "text": "- Suggested fix: Generate a unique request ID, pass it with the broadcast, record it plus a monotonic generation in the atomically written snapshot, and poll until that exact ID appears under a finite deadline. Alternatively remove the old file before a checked broadcast and require a strictly newer file identity, while retaining IDs to avoid races between concurrent callers.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "14f7c61495183bbe1f054e6f7b38183937d4dd8910bc0dbd977d6189f40d2946"
      },
      {
        "line": 18725,
        "text": "- Validation: Exercise ordinary, delayed, paused, ignored, stopped, concurrent, and restarted game/setup receivers with prepopulated valid files. Require only the matching new generation to succeed, stale and cross-request files never to satisfy the call, timeout within the bound, and atomic complete JSON at every accepted read.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "733d7943280b731d3c314db2b4ed47eff34a446a2b28e9632a553c2271d10bf3"
      },
      {
        "line": 18726,
        "text": "- Additional location (R1-CHUNK-0546): `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/helpers/test_helpers.ps1:L1331-L1357,L1546-L1565,L1743-L1749` in shared launcher-stall diagnostics and game/setup introspection reads",
        "line_lf_sha256": "33b8031f85c8ca974271bc5de685946c4981c22154e901041baa7352b3867147"
      },
      {
        "line": 18727,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0546): The PowerShell helpers repeat the same uncorrelated contract for both single- and multi-device callers: broadcast, sleep exactly 800 ms, and accept any object-shaped final file without first removing it, recording its generation, or checking a request ID. The automation watcher also uses whichever `setup_introspect.json` survives to declare the game stuck at the launcher. A dropped broadcast, slow receiver, stopped process, prior device session, or concurrent request can therefore return stale game/setup state or turn an old setup screen into a current test failure despite bounded ADB calls.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "7d53f1f5ea1fa6fa84b39e37a9ab5a486edad32a23720dce33a3af7f205b93df"
      },
      {
        "line": 18728,
        "text": "- Additional validation (R1-CHUNK-0546): Run every helper and the watcher with prepopulated valid snapshots, dropped and delayed broadcasts, process restart, both maintained serials, concurrent request IDs, and old setup-screen state followed by current gameplay. Require only the matching post-request generation to satisfy a read or launcher-stall decision, exact serial correlation, atomic JSON, and a finite diagnostic timeout for every absent response.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "ba3151ed72ce1688d9be014be85a0180325b45660dfd37ad64f5a488750cab67"
      },
      {
        "line": 18729,
        "text": "- Resolution: Pending",
        "line_lf_sha256": "9283e8d2b02a60dd95afcd32c737e52c949ba2cc15180e9d8967d03ca1e1d976"
      },
      {
        "line": 18730,
        "text": "",
        "line_lf_sha256": "01ba4719c80b6fe911b091a7c05124b64eeece964e09c058ef8f9805daca546b"
      },
      {
        "line": 19800,
        "text": "### BR-0662: P2 - Keep mailbox assertions active in registered builds",
        "line_lf_sha256": "77dfa60cf988f8b0bfcaf85a12577dd4db2ddc262bfb44cce442bcd7ccd409a6"
      },
      {
        "line": 19801,
        "text": "",
        "line_lf_sha256": "01ba4719c80b6fe911b091a7c05124b64eeece964e09c058ef8f9805daca546b"
      },
      {
        "line": 19802,
        "text": "- [ ] OPEN",
        "line_lf_sha256": "b51f310dfc99aa47b58cba7fed1dcfd6b13bab97a2e98b391a97d32ca57cef17"
      },
      {
        "line": 19803,
        "text": "- Type: defect",
        "line_lf_sha256": "c35c251f5479984ecc3bbf292b2b96bf30c6e0e55e1ed672b025c4eb0125e918"
      },
      {
        "line": 19804,
        "text": "- Confidence: high",
        "line_lf_sha256": "666adfafe63b7fd4e579910b19eecf81f6e25bc211a7165f4bffdf849ca7e334"
      },
      {
        "line": 19805,
        "text": "- Category: test-gap/false-pass",
        "line_lf_sha256": "15ee323aee824e6ac25f6eae8ce317f2a7c864c09d7319f73f1ee989d6b124f9"
      },
      {
        "line": 19806,
        "text": "- Found by: R1-CHUNK-0554, R1-CHUNK-0558, R1-CHUNK-0560",
        "line_lf_sha256": "f6cc40cea4c8add4800c14bbd95de30a0ce5640d671e8a954cb2eee2e91aa3b9"
      },
      {
        "line": 19807,
        "text": "- Location: `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/tests/test_android_axis_mailbox.cpp:L3-L165` in all behavioral oracles",
        "line_lf_sha256": "52003e97d1511e3395aa2faaa418c85912b98e6b89f738a779ac4f909b03d868"
      },
      {
        "line": 19808,
        "text": "- Related: `d1/maths/CMakeLists.txt:L98-L102,L198-L206`; `d2/maths/CMakeLists.txt:L103-L107,L214-L221`; `run-windows-build.ps1:L2-L7`; `run-linux-build.sh:L4-L23`; `android/helpers/test_host_platform.ps1:L472-L510`",
        "line_lf_sha256": "7a89aa361fa2241d055c550a4905ac1f4bc398e7459ec8fbd5fab7fa390871c0"
      },
      {
        "line": 19809,
        "text": "- Evidence: Every behavioral check in the focused test is the standard C/C++ `assert` macro; outside those assertions, `main` only invokes mailbox operations, prints `android axis mailbox tests passed`, and returns zero. Both paired CMake files build and register that source without a test-specific assertion or check facility and without removing `NDEBUG`. The maintained Windows and Linux build wrappers default to `RelWithDebInfo`, and the shared host-test build helper invokes those defaults. Retained paired D1 and D2 RelWithDebInfo caches specify `/DNDEBUG`; both resulting executables printed the success banner and exited zero. Consequently their registered executions check no return value, snapshot field, generation relationship, diagnostic count, transition, automation state, or batch behavior under the normal build path.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "8dc83586f72b71301d9bb2bbf2c593be82723f12ba51d7d0bd837a1ec17c2375"
      },
      {
        "line": 19810,
        "text": "- Trigger: Introduce any mailbox regression covered only by the assigned assertions, then build and run the paired native suite through the default host-test path or another Release/RelWithDebInfo configuration that defines `NDEBUG`",
        "line_lf_sha256": "0167b3d7717dfc4887bcbd7442c0825da81f1c2cca4999428e8b0a06d09d087e"
      },
      {
        "line": 19811,
        "text": "- Impact: The sole purpose-built axis-mailbox regression target reports PASS while exercising none of its advertised oracles, so failures in input coalescing, transitions, automation release, stale acknowledgements, or paired game-facing state can enter optimized builds undetected.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "178ecfa0e5ffe59aef1b6b2d3375dfd21652c3eda3131936e3001ffc7cba99af"
      },
      {
        "line": 19812,
        "text": "- Expected: Registered test behavior and failure status are independent of production assertion policy; every oracle remains active and returns nonzero in Debug, Release, and RelWithDebInfo builds.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "b2cafe9e3c00141e01c85b6ff524e6faa09b31a1a87a8daf2b6c14b3f964d147"
      },
      {
        "line": 19813,
        "text": "- Suggested fix: Replace standard `assert` calls with an always-enabled test check that reports the failed expression and returns nonzero, or configure this target explicitly to keep assertions enabled on every supported compiler while ensuring production targets retain their intended policy. Prefer the explicit test harness so future build flags cannot silently erase coverage.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "697c7b7a36f0f6d9145c06f68e8f6e976040bb0788ebcb768b83e0cfeecb19d9"
      },
      {
        "line": 19814,
        "text": "- Validation: Build and run the target for paired D1 and D2 under Debug, Release, and RelWithDebInfo; deliberately break each covered mailbox contract in turn and require every configuration to fail with the responsible oracle, then restore the implementation and require all configurations plus CTest to pass. Inspect compile commands to prove no configuration removes the test checks.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "38286ab95268ad1dcb1b65149ec2bdcbbbb9c7411f04e1fe21e2476509214233"
      },
      {
        "line": 19815,
        "text": "- Additional location (R1-CHUNK-0555): `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/tests/test_escort_exit_policy.c:L3-L12`; `d2/maths/CMakeLists.txt:L139-L141,L223-L224`",
        "line_lf_sha256": "a046e96463f2fcb6e25a28609c7253f7e7782fc285b1f75ee90360d9bde747c1"
      },
      {
        "line": 19816,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0555): The registered D2 escort-exit target repeats the same optimized-build false pass on a smaller surface: all four policy oracles use standard `assert`, while its CMake target has no always-enabled check or `NDEBUG` override. The retained RelWithDebInfo executable prints `escort exit policy tests passed` and exits zero under the same `/DNDEBUG` configuration, so it validates none of the external-exit, trigger fallback, or absent-exit cases. The neighboring owner-policy test uses an explicit always-enabled `CHECK` macro, demonstrating the intended robust pattern within the assigned scope.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "b197879b2ef34daebd84fe1668c46d1273a396f0dbb063ec724ca024b770c0ae"
      },
      {
        "line": 19817,
        "text": "- Additional validation (R1-CHUNK-0555): Include `test_escort_exit_policy` in BR-0662's Debug, Release, and RelWithDebInfo mutation matrix, requiring every one of its four policy regressions to fail in every configuration while the owner-policy target retains its always-enabled behavior.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "a2f8cadf24e7bf01649fa381d23521721d2d4d8d88fa6c129c599d9c04b96bf7"
      },
      {
        "line": 19818,
        "text": "- Additional location (R1-CHUNK-0560): `android/tests/test_hud_layout.c:L1-L94`; `d1/maths/CMakeLists.txt:L59-L61,L238`; `d2/maths/CMakeLists.txt:L60-L62,L257` in HUD helper oracles and paired registration",
        "line_lf_sha256": "ccd0df6a7ed7abefced71f702b6180d0b78797393321349f33fb24ff70c37f35"
      },
      {
        "line": 19819,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0560): Every frozen font-control and rectangle-layout oracle uses standard `assert`, and `main` otherwise only calls the helpers and returns zero. Both paired targets inherit the same optimized configuration without restoring assertions. Retained D1 and D2 RelWithDebInfo caches define `NDEBUG`, and both executables exited zero while therefore checking none of the visible-character, overlap, edge, containment, empty, null or padding expectations. The live boss-bar cases repeat standard assertions and remain subject to the same false pass.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "139b8a6f20c4c7bf9fae0d3eeb82dd3170987d7614d1921503797a025c354be4"
      },
      {
        "line": 19820,
        "text": "- Additional validation (R1-CHUNK-0560): Include paired `test_hud_layout` in the existing Debug, Release and RelWithDebInfo mutation matrix, break every font-control, rectangle and live boss-width expectation independently, and require nonzero field-specific failure under all configurations after converting the target to always-enabled checks.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "15bac197000a755e65e4c6aa3bcb0fbda936d38ec901d5a5e08f224c011ced50"
      },
      {
        "line": 19821,
        "text": "- Additional location (R1-CHUNK-0562): `android/tests/test_route_snapshot.cpp:L1101-L1212`; `d1/maths/CMakeLists.txt:L114-L122`; `d2/maths/CMakeLists.txt:L119-L127`",
        "line_lf_sha256": "fd612212aa5943d2430c223c370d48ca33434a673814eea79c639be358195be3"
      },
      {
        "line": 19822,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0562): Every oracle in the assigned snapshot-summary, repeated-hash, opening-wall, automap-fingerprint, changed-state, topology-change and invalid-view tail uses standard `assert`; the final two statements only print success and return zero. Both paired targets inherit the default optimized configuration without restoring assertions. Retained D1 and D2 RelWithDebInfo caches define `NDEBUG`, and both executables printed `route snapshot tests passed` and exited zero while all assigned checks were compiled out. BR-0396 separately records that neither executable is registered with CTest, so direct optimized execution also provides no behavioral protection.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "a01a92ba332076631eaac8b5ecf4670374d652e25c2467c2678ceffda055a9c9"
      },
      {
        "line": 19823,
        "text": "- Additional validation (R1-CHUNK-0562): Include paired `test_route_snapshot` in the existing Debug, Release and RelWithDebInfo mutation matrix after registration under BR-0396, independently break summary fields, each fingerprint partition, wall state, topology identity and invalid-view rejection, and require a field-specific nonzero result in every configuration.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "23d8f1c3490b9b495d82c59b0142628e1c3a6488172f65362a35e732750889f5"
      },
      {
        "line": 19824,
        "text": "- Resolution: Pending",
        "line_lf_sha256": "9283e8d2b02a60dd95afcd32c737e52c949ba2cc15180e9d8967d03ca1e1d976"
      },
      {
        "line": 19825,
        "text": "",
        "line_lf_sha256": "01ba4719c80b6fe911b091a7c05124b64eeece964e09c058ef8f9805daca546b"
      },
      {
        "line": 13736,
        "text": "### BR-0233: P2 - Publish headless outputs only after a verified write",
        "line_lf_sha256": "38b1707aacdfcba2a2c8282992d2965015f9dc75f6a230a420164cb794ff2c6a"
      },
      {
        "line": 13737,
        "text": "",
        "line_lf_sha256": "01ba4719c80b6fe911b091a7c05124b64eeece964e09c058ef8f9805daca546b"
      },
      {
        "line": 13738,
        "text": "- [ ] OPEN",
        "line_lf_sha256": "b51f310dfc99aa47b58cba7fed1dcfd6b13bab97a2e98b391a97d32ca57cef17"
      },
      {
        "line": 13739,
        "text": "- Type: defect",
        "line_lf_sha256": "c35c251f5479984ecc3bbf292b2b96bf30c6e0e55e1ed672b025c4eb0125e918"
      },
      {
        "line": 13740,
        "text": "- Confidence: high",
        "line_lf_sha256": "666adfafe63b7fd4e579910b19eecf81f6e25bc211a7165f4bffdf849ca7e334"
      },
      {
        "line": 13741,
        "text": "- Category: correctness/output-integrity",
        "line_lf_sha256": "fe431cc20b48fbe861455a7ed35ce53132233d4a952e6546c347067cbfd64333"
      },
      {
        "line": 13742,
        "text": "- Found by: R1-CHUNK-0123, R1-CHUNK-0135",
        "line_lf_sha256": "294f722686a8c6b7501cbb043fd8fe640ee5345a724fbb8c4ae8f0816dadf464"
      },
      {
        "line": 13743,
        "text": "- Location: `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/app/src/main/cpp/headless/headless_metadata_dump_main.cpp:L1018-L1051` in both metadata-output branches and `android/app/src/main/cpp/shared/input_demo_state_trace.cpp:L646-L791` in state-trace publication",
        "line_lf_sha256": "5ad902adcbccc2f60aed01534641bbf1f73c20e972e12651864d95dbe87128b7"
      },
      {
        "line": 13744,
        "text": "- Related: `android/app/src/main/cpp/shared/input_demo_hooks_shared.c:L665-L694`, `android/app/src/main/cpp/headless/input_demo_headless_main.cpp:L220-L245`, `android/tests/run_input_demo_replay.ps1:L1607-L1662`, `android/tests/regenerate_all_mission_metadata_host.ps1:L519-L523`, `android/tests/test_coop_start_fanout_mapset.ps1:L81-L89`, `android/tests/test_secret_area_baseline.ps1:L153-L159`, and `android/tests/test_counterstrike_level2_trigger21_route.ps1:L29-L34`",
        "line_lf_sha256": "ae0c128d3df7c7fd83e12ff54046ef90170b743630b39aa6d81bb30f8558bc53"
      },
      {
        "line": 13745,
        "text": "- Evidence: Each branch opens the caller's final output path before building the dump, so opening immediately truncates any prior valid generation. It then writes the generated JSON directly to that public stream. If level discovery or loading sets `secret_area_dump_failed`, the function returns after the write and leaves the failed generation at the final path. On the nominal path it checks `!stream` before any explicit flush or close, prints an OK marker, and returns zero while the destructor performs the first close; an error reported only while flushing or closing therefore cannot change the exit status. The maintained callers trust a zero process exit before reading or canonicalizing the file and do not provide publication or filesystem-fault coverage.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "524e4280b02415a6492ccb097b0f2117e87a2e11b8967570e40db95afbaf70b2"
      },
      {
        "line": 13746,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0135): State-trace start likewise opens the caller's final path with truncation before the metadata record is durable, ignores the initial `fflush` result, writes every frame directly to that visible file, and returns no status from stop while `reset_session` discards `fclose` failure. A frame flush failure disables tracing but leaves the partial replacement in place, and process interruption has the same visible result. Thus a failed diagnostic run can destroy a prior useful trace or leave an existing but incomplete generation for the replay wrapper, whose focused tests cover content comparison but not transactional publication or filesystem faults.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "8e2e658280f31cafdcc71996fc3a5d9e6262d5e0d927a82e75f8e57a41eb7792"
      },
      {
        "line": 13747,
        "text": "- Trigger: Regenerate metadata or a replay state trace over an existing valid output while generation fails, storage fills, a delayed write or close error occurs, or the process is interrupted after opening the final path",
        "line_lf_sha256": "84e4b80da1375c9634a63320c429e3bc5829c8684515a887ec75c5612367af59"
      },
      {
        "line": 13748,
        "text": "- Impact: A failed run can destroy the previous valid metadata or diagnostic trace and leave a failed, truncated, or empty replacement at its canonical pathname; a flush- or close-only failure can also print OK and exit zero, allowing automation to accept or propagate incomplete output.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "8df2b78cdec32e166aeb3dc8b25ea0938143cf541af53b9c46e90a7d8c63d26f"
      },
      {
        "line": 13749,
        "text": "- Expected: A successful exit publishes each requested output as one completely generated, flushed, and closed document, while any generation or I/O failure exits nonzero, emits no OK marker, and preserves the prior valid output.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "dea98e1829accdfcf573d32553827f7c7a0272aff764e4e9b4d3569349ca4a1d"
      },
      {
        "line": 13750,
        "text": "- Suggested fix: Finish generation before touching the destination, write to a uniquely owned sibling temporary file, explicitly flush and close while checking every result, and atomically replace the destination only after generation and I/O both succeed. Remove only the owned temporary on failure.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "185cce0de88746b6d253049ac7e39450bea7b58c49551d4dd0db0b3b2b74fbae"
      },
      {
        "line": 13751,
        "text": "- Validation: Preseed valid metadata and state-trace destinations and inject level-scan failure, trace-start and frame failure, output open failure, partial write, flush failure, close failure, disk full, interruption, and replacement failure in every output mode. Assert every failure exits nonzero without an OK marker and leaves each prior file byte-for-byte intact; assert success publishes exactly one parseable expected document per request and no temporary residue.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "a295151bac7199e79efa5abed657e8133089a76b5c3faa461d57eb5847c2e192"
      },
      {
        "line": 13752,
        "text": "- Additional location (R1-CHUNK-0164): `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/app/src/main/cpp/shared/game_automate.cpp:L575-L708` in automation result and step-log publication",
        "line_lf_sha256": "724bbd8600af550f1720e85298642f84e94cfb92a823f21584eb2dbc2cddb615"
      },
      {
        "line": 13753,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0164): The automation result writer uses a sibling temporary file but ignores every `fprintf`, `fflush`, and `fclose` result, returns no publication status, and deletes the temporary silently when rename fails. Its caller then logs PASS or FAIL, clears active execution, and cannot distinguish a durable terminal result from an absent or partial one. The JSONL logger truncates its canonical file at every load and likewise ignores each append, flush, and terminal close result. A delayed storage error can therefore leave no authoritative result and an incomplete diagnostic log even though the engine has irreversibly stopped the script, so maintained watchers reach their outer timeout instead of receiving the actual outcome.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "40b755c664807d207696828f65ec19fcf1c8203d1575393d64268b6943d773c5"
      },
      {
        "line": 13754,
        "text": "- Additional validation (R1-CHUNK-0164): Preseed a valid result and log, then inject result and log open failure, partial `fprintf`, flush failure, close failure, disk full, interruption, and rename failure for PASS, FAIL, and `LAUNCHER_CONTINUE`. Require a checked publication failure to reach the runner promptly, no script to claim durable completion without its matching artifact, preservation of the prior complete generation where appropriate, and no partial JSON or JSONL at the canonical paths.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "ff1714a3da099db0c4449d9660bd46f41cf7e313611d1577addf9bcfcaecf3ed"
      },
      {
        "line": 13755,
        "text": "- Additional location (R1-CHUNK-0280): `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:d2/main/inferno.c:L230-L253,L499-L505` in classic-demo dump dispatch and `d2/main/newdemo.c:L4204-L4324` in JSONL publication",
        "line_lf_sha256": "2ea552a1c02aadc9dcf9783b08307a56be5e2292d377da0131d2fbd57d286695"
      },
      {
        "line": 13756,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0280): The command accepts a caller-selected final output, and `newdemo_dump_json` opens it with `fopen(output_path, \"wb\")` before mounting or opening the input. It writes and flushes the header and every decoded frame directly to that visible file. Although it detects explicit write, flush, and close failures and returns nonzero, every missing-input, mount, header, frame-decode, write, close, or interruption failure occurs after the previous output was truncated and leaves the empty or partial replacement in place. The maintained conversion records cover successful distinct-path runs but no prior-output preservation or filesystem fault.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "f35e2447a038815bbffe5b8d14596435138b6c6589529e82385de14e09537d6a"
      },
      {
        "line": 13757,
        "text": "- Additional validation (R1-CHUNK-0280): Preseed a valid JSONL destination, then run missing, unreadable, malformed, and frame-truncated demos plus injected write, flush, close, disk-full, interruption, and replacement failures. Require nonzero status, byte-for-byte preservation of the prior valid output, no partial canonical output or temporary residue, and one complete parseable replacement only after successful decode and checked close.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "7c907f75f3406b98022d71bb01873156429afe11dfe82774a225ce1715488cc9"
      },
      {
        "line": 13758,
        "text": "- Additional location (R1-CHUNK-0490): `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/helpers/regenerate_all_mission_metadata_host.ps1:L180-L208,L576-L586,L653-L656` in normalized JSON and checked-in regression publication",
        "line_lf_sha256": "a2fdbe3dbd1b7402068f247a3a64831be93e39931d463247fb3a98c07b96818b"
      },
      {
        "line": 13759,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0490): The PowerShell writer calls `File.WriteAllText` on its final metadata path, and successful built-in and archive scans then use forced `Copy-Item` directly onto each checked-in regression JSON. Neither path uses a unique sibling, checked flush/close, atomic replacement, or rollback. Interruption, storage exhaustion, or a copy failure after opening the destination can therefore truncate a prior valid regression file; the per-archive catch records failure only after that canonical file may already have been damaged.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "432b63c17853aa4b7d7263bdd608ed6e59cac2b205ad725bf8f5063d9caf7c92"
      },
      {
        "line": 13760,
        "text": "- Additional validation (R1-CHUNK-0490): Preseed valid host metadata and checked-in regression files, inject partial write/copy, disk-full, access, interruption, flush/close, and replacement failures, and run concurrent publishers. Require byte-identical prior files on every failure and one complete normalized atomic replacement only after all I/O succeeds.",
        "line_lf_sha256": "1703b90e64aa03ae75729feddea1b897a348e7fe9a2a1f1496496c4aa5d97812"
      },
      {
        "line": 13761,
        "text": "- Additional location (R1-CHUNK-0494): `c01d8fe4686c63d931b1e543a6305bbafaa944a9:android/helpers/collect_crash.ps1:L17-L31,L36-L117,L119-L143` in crash-report directory replacement and completion reporting",
        "line_lf_sha256": "7035e0ee5c47200dc336362efd12ba037657e69f5efe9125344ea4b133032c6c"
      },
      {
        "line": 13762,
        "text": "- Additional evidence (R1-CHUNK-0494): The collector recursively deletes `temp/crash_report` and creates its replacement before even checking whether an emulator exists, so the ordinary no-device error destroys the prior complete diagnostic generation. It then writes logcat, tombstone or bugreport, private debug logs, introspection, automation state, and metadata directly into that public directory while suppressing or ignoring every ADB capture failure. ## GLES probe restoration and transaction candidate ownership checkpoint, 2026-10-09

Read whole current GLES header and bounded probe779-955. Android macro facade redirects buffer and texture transitions, array capture and draw path while preserving desktop includes. Probe rejects an active external shader, saves arrays/matrix/flat state and actual GL bindings, draws VBO offsets0/3, requires white center and batch equivalence, checks deleting bound ARRAY_BUFFER resets both actual/tracked binding and preserves COPY_READ_BUFFER through restoration. Restores captured arrays, matrices, current program, framebuffer, texture, viewport, clear color, enabled blend/cull/depth/scissor and prior array/copy-read bindings. Do not replace shared tracking or infer a missing restoration merely from a raw-call search

Whole current2D batch124lines uses fixed1536vertex storage with mode/full-buffer flush and six-vertex rectangle mapping. Ordinary caller contract supports two line/four rectangle vertices; probe compares immediate/batched pixels for empty, line, rectangle and mixed modes and exceeds capacity with800iterations. Probe explicitly binds ARRAY_BUFFER0 before client pointers, disables texturing array, restores blend functions; enclosing GLES probe restores broader state. This is inspected source acceptance, not a test/probe execution or general acceptance of arbitrary caller counts

Whole transaction header65lines confirms three targets/16MiB each and retained original backup contract. Re-read actual production1-45,100-230,250-340,365-455,455-539 (already whole-read) corroborates concrete candidate ownership defect: write_private_file writes caller-owned cleanup pathname before config_open succeeds; after100EEXIST or non-EEXIST open failure it returns with nonempty candidate. Both batch/atomic-replace failure goto cleanup_staged, which removes nonempty temporary/backup path without acquisition proof. Path truncation can also leave a nonempty candidate. ## Current native projection ownership and bounded producer checkpoint, 2026-10-09

Read seven actual current projection ranges and eight producer owners. Recovery projects borrowed items/count and live object storage, with actual append reserving before increment and all-or-nothing pointer replacement on allocation failure. No copy/synchronization provided by count/data accessor. Engine-thread dump reads differ from direct UI/JNI reads; preserve existing BR-0029 snapshot ownership boundary, no inferred coherence from request-generation atomics. Recovery raw admission/world accounting broader contracts are not proven by these bounded reads

Flyout actor state zero-initialized and bounds checked against MAX_PLAYERS; exit_age rejects invalid/unexited player and checks world generation. Actual fixed MAX_PLAYERS state and borrowed native fields match projection. Projection iterates N_players without its own maximum unlike multiplayer inventory loop; ordinary native N_players admission is a precondition, no new malformed-input or index allegation established here. Movie producer traverses native window stack and matches exact callbacks before borrowing movie data; snapshots not lifetime-independent and direct JNI ownership remains existing boundary

Readiness producer uses80byte static text, clears state before display admission, clamps incomplete progress0..999 and snprintf-bounds messages. Getter returns borrowed text/state. Music projection initializes PATH_MAX title and zero/default scalar outputs; current list size-query accepts nonnegative <=SONGS_TRACK_LIST_MAX_BYTES, allocates size+1, requires exact second return and array parse. Whole shared JSON writer counts escaped output with SIZE_MAX/INT_MAX and capacity checks, returns failure/empty buffer for inadequate supplied capacity, preserves shared builtin/redbook/custom iteration. Preserve dynamic exact-size improvement; JSON allocation after buffer acquisition still needs GQR-0173 scoped cleanup/containment, no resurrected fixed-track-list truncation issue

Content trace uses current D1-in-D2/native stats, controller projection normalizes sampled frame with denominator guards and explicit diagnostic samples/generations. Menu projections use zero-initialized copied states; PhysFS search list released after allocating JSON/string pushes, so exceptional exit before freeList remains current GQR-0173 acquired-resource acceptance. Mission owner accessor borrows catalog string or base default; context_json static allocating snapshot is a separate consumer contract, no immutable snapshot guarantee inferred. Current GPU capability and MSAA parse projection invokes allocating/throwing parse; existing containment owner applies. Producer bodies for stats/GPU/axis/menu beyond current saved coverage still require final reconciliation before gate2 complete

Canonical GQF-0186 OPEN/GQR-0173 TODO44MEDIUM current acceptance read exactly: production allocation/parse/dump faults must not cross C ABI, leak acquired malloc/PhysFS resources, emit partial output or label stale result current; coordinate separate publication/correlation and admission owners. Historical expanded replay/weapon/route acceptance remains unchanged. No new finding, status, rating, build/test/runtime credit or inherited saving

## Final projection copies and maintained catalog/flyout fixture checkpoint, 2026-10-09

Current asset stats getters copy complete structs or zero missing active custom state; introspection supplies real stack structs, no uninitialized-stats claim. Axis mailbox getter locks shared mutex for complete copied diagnostics and pending-axis accounting. Automation axis probe is an ordinary copied struct with no locking, so engine ownership remains required; do not broaden mailbox locking to all Controls/probe state. Paired kconfig samples FrameTime into Controls.sampled_frame_time before control events, matching projection normalization. Menu interaction publication bounds region_count, copies under interaction lock, releases atomic index; get_state acquires same lock and copies. Separate menu-scale getter reads native globals without lock; preserve distinction between copied values and a coherent cross-subsystem snapshot

GPU report getter locks report_mutex but snprintf truncates to supplied unsigned capacity, has no required-size or failure result. Introspection8192byte parse is throwing; producer dump may allocate and publication uses PID-only sibling, checked fwrite/close and rename. No broad unbounded-vendor-string claim admitted; existing GQR0173 must verify length/complete-JSON admission plus parse/allocation failure containment and coordinate BR0233 owned complete publication. Preserving mutex and checked-close improvements is part of acceptance

Whole maintained track writer fixture exercises exact query/fill, escaped names, undersized buffer guard bytes and catalogs100/128/256/1024 including >32768bytes. Actual CMake700-709 compiles production writer and registers CTest; previously bound extraction assertion-retention loop applies to test_ target. Source assertions alone are not a runtime pass or production allocation-fault oracle. Current upstream flyout fixture1763-1819 checks staggered/absent actors, duplicate-clock monotonicity, late catch-up, finish/disconnect/secret exit, teardown/reset and gameplay object/clock/SIM RNG preservation. Read slice starts after setup, so no whole-fixture/configuration/runtime credit inferred

These reads complete focused producer traces for the identified projections without expanding the frozen assigned scope. Remaining consolidation must revalidate all saved current identities, bind/read complete nonempty current context deltas, reconcile canonical publication/acquisition owner and highest live rating, and distinguish fixture limits before gates/report/publication. No canonical status or rating changes here

## Current graphics safety and production directory fixture deltas, 2026-10-09

Completely read/bound graphics safety31hunk current delta and whole added directory fixture216line delta. Current safety delegates simulation blocking to pause owner; adds live preview durable record before GL apply, revision/quiet-period admission, all-off/accepted finish check, retry-first-run policy and completed-frame withholding. Preserve these current repairs and independent UI watchdog order, no historical stop_time/pause_owned claim carried forward. Source diff adds no runtime or whole899+line implementation credit. Existing checked-lock admission follow-up remains separate from renderer/pause improvements

Directory fixture actual production inclusion and always-enabled require are preserved, including persistent/one-shot sync failure, failed directory close, atomic/batch existing/absent targets, rollback original inventory, /proc/F_GETFD actual descriptor counts, read/open/relative-path controls. Not an exclusive-name acquisition/collision oracle; acceptance gap from candidate cleanup remains explicit. Whole diff reconfirms already saved current whole fixture evidence, does not multiply physical whole credit

Focused current caller/producer trace and maintained fixture/registration inspection are complete for diagnosis gates2/3. They establish source behavior and explicit coverage gaps, not successful execution or complete acceptance of existing findings. Gate4 still requires final full-context delta/owner consolidation; gate5 publication and independent audit pending. Frozen assigned scope unchanged/zero original effect; external product work preserved
